### Realignment / membership notes (brief)

- **California, Stanford, and SMU** compete as ACC football members after joining the conference in **2024** (third ACC football season in 2026).
- **Notre Dame** is an ACC member in most sports but is **not** a full ACC football member (independent football with an ACC scheduling agreement). **No Notre Dame research file is included in this package**, and Notre Dame is **not** counted among the seventeen ACC football members below.
- This package covers only the seventeen schools with team research files present in `/workspace/acc-2026/`.

---

## 2. Methodology and Primary Source Types

Research for each team was compiled from publicly available sources accessed on or about **September 4–5, 2026**. Primary source types used across the conference package:

1. **Official athletics department rosters** (school `.edu` / athletics sites) — primary authority for jersey numbers, names, positions, hometowns/high schools, and academic eligibility class when published.
2. **Official or beat-reported depth charts** — e.g., Boston College Week-1 PDF, Duke Week-1 chart, Florida State Week-0 game-day chart, Georgia Tech Week-1 game notes, NC State chart vs Virginia (PackInsider), Pitt Narduzzi Notebook, Syracuse Week-1 program two-deep, Virginia Week-0 chart, Wake Forest Week-1 program chart.
3. **Ourlads.com** depth charts and numeric/alphabetical rosters — secondary depth and hometown cross-checks (update stamps vary by school; often June–September 2026).
4. **Beat / local media depth charts and coaching comments** — e.g., Bear Insider (California), Greenville News / The State (Clemson), Cards On SI / Courier Journal (Louisville), Canes On SI (Miami), Inside Carolina / Heel Tough (North Carolina), Dave Campbell’s Texas Football / On3 (SMU), SI Stanford / Miami opener coverage, Virginia Tech On SI / Tech Sideline.
5. **Recruiting databases and class trackers** — 247Sports, On3, ESPN recruiting, CFBTrack, school signing-day releases — used **only** when a specific rating was publicly verified for a named player.
6. **Secondary cross-checks** — ESPN / CBS roster pages, Wikipedia season pages (portal/schedule context), school game notes PDFs where cited in team files.

**Policy enforced throughout:** If a recruiting star, high-school class year, or starter designation could not be verified in the sources consulted, the field is marked **Not listed** or the chart is labeled **projected**. No ratings or starter assignments were invented.

---

## 3. Cross-Conference Caveats

Readers should treat the attached materials as a **research snapshot**, not as an official Atlantic Coast Conference or NCAA publication. The following limitations apply conference-wide:

| Caveat | Implication |
| --- | --- |
| **Stars incomplete for transfers and upperclassmen** | Composite / consensus stars are filled primarily for verified 2026 (and some earlier) high-school signees and a minority of returners/transfers with published bios. Most portal arrivals and multi-year veterans are **Not listed**. |
| **Many depth charts are projected, not official** | Boston College, Duke, Florida State, Georgia Tech, NC State, Pittsburgh, Syracuse, Virginia, and Wake Forest have official / official-via-beat Week-1 (or Week-0) two-deeps. Virginia Tech is **MIXED** (coach-confirmed QB/OL; remainder projected). California, Clemson, Louisville, Miami (FL), North Carolina, SMU, and Stanford charts are primarily **PROJECTED** (Ourlads and/or beat). Clemson’s QB (Christopher Vizzina) is coach-confirmed even though the full chart is projected. |
| **Jersey mismatches** | Numbers frequently differ across athletics sites, Ourlads, and media charts (shared numbers on large FBS rosters; media vs official conflicts). Prefer the school athletics roster for jersey when sources conflict (explicitly noted for NC State and others). |
| **Position-battle volatility** | Extensive **OR** / co-starter listings (especially Boston College, Duke, Florida State, Georgia Tech, Pittsburgh) mean Week-1 actual lineups may diverge from published boards. |
| **Roster churn / injuries** | Portal turnover, mid-camp injuries, and eligibility rulings can change counts after the research date. |
| **Hometown / HS quirks** | Occasional official-site anomalies (blank HS fields, mismatched city/school pairs, spelling variants) are preserved as published rather than “corrected.” |
| **Notre Dame exclusion** | Notre Dame football is independent / not a full ACC football member and is omitted from this package. |
| **Miami naming** | **Miami (FL)** denotes the University of Miami Hurricanes (ACC) — **not** Miami (OH) / MAC. |

## 5. How to Read the Attached Team Files

Each school file in `/workspace/acc-2026/` follows a consistent research structure:

1. **Header** — Conference affiliation, head coach, home venue (when stated), access/research date.
2. **Sources table** — URLs and access dates for every primary source used for that school.
3. **Starter Depth Chart** — Offense, defense, and specialists, with an explicit **projected vs official** caveat at the top of the section.
4. **Full Roster** — Player tables with columns for Name, Stars, Class Year, Position, High School / Hometown, and Notes (when present). Gaps are preserved as **Not listed**.
5. **Data gaps / caveats** — School-specific limitations (injuries, eligibility murk, jersey conflicts, OR competitions, etc.).

### Companion documents in this package

| File | Contents |
| --- | --- |
| `00-executive-overview.md` | This overview (scope, methodology, caveats, snapshot table) |
| `01-starter-depth-charts-summary.md` | Consolidated starter charts only, with roster-linked Stars / Class Year / HS-Hometown when present in source tables |
| `ACC-2026-Football-Rosters-Complete.md` | Master volume: overview + starter summary + full text of all seventeen team files (alphabetical by school) |
| `boston-college.md` … `wake-forest.md` | Individual school research files (source of truth for player-level detail) |

### Reading conventions

- Depth-chart **Status** labels (**OFFICIAL**, **MIXED**, **PROJECTED**) appear at the top of each school section in the starter summary.
- **Not listed** means the field was not verified — it does **not** mean “unrated” in a recruiting-service sense, nor that the player is unimportant.
- **Class Year** may mean academic eligibility (Fr/So/Jr/Sr / R-Sr / 5th / Gr / Post-Bacc) as published on athletics rosters, or HS recruiting/signing year when that is how the school file recorded it — see each file’s roster preamble.
- Prefer **official athletics spelling** when media spellings diverge (documented in individual caveats).
- Starter-summary enrichment pulls Stars / Class Year / HS-Hometown **only** from that school’s full roster table when a name match is found; unmatched depth-chart names retain **Not listed** gaps.
- **Miami (FL)** denotes the University of Miami — ACC — **not** Miami University (Oxford, Ohio / MAC).
- **Notre Dame** is omitted (not a full ACC football member; no file in this package).

## Data gaps

1. **Recruiting stars** missing for vast majority of returners and transfers (marked Not listed). Verified stars mainly for 2026 HS signees from CFBTrack/SI/Heights.
2. Depth PDF vs roster **class-year abbreviations** sometimes disagree (e.g., Crounse R-So. on PDF vs R-Jr. on roster; Pruitt R-Fr. on PDF vs R-So. on roster) — roster page used for Full Roster class years.
3. Ashton Cunningham weight listed 170 on roster / 165 on depth PDF.
4. No comprehensive On3/ESPN star dump for entire roster in one public page at research time.

1. **No OFFICIAL** athletics Week 1 depth chart (confirmed by SI preview / media notes). Projection only.
2. Bear Insider vs Ourlads disagree on OL LT, TE order, secondary nickel/CB — BI preferred (newer).

## Data Gaps

1. **No OFFICIAL published two-deep** — blank chart before Stanford; all starters PROJECTED (Canes On SI / Ourlads).
2. **SI "Bobby Pruitt"** vs roster **Cam Pruitt** naming mismatch at WLB backup.
3. **Ourlads DE order** differs slightly from SI (Lightfoot vs Blount as LDE) — SI Week 1 projection prioritized.
4. **Recruiting stars** verified only for 2026 OL signees Cantwell (5*), Congdon (4*), Ervin (4*), Pickett/Woodrow/Sparks (3*). Most roster = Not listed.
5. **Special teams** kicker spelling: SI "Jake Olsen" vs roster "Jack Olsen".
6. Dual jersey numbers common; listed once per unique player name from athletics site.

## Data Gaps

1. **Duke Scott jersey:** PackInsider official DC table showed #4 for RB Duke Scott; gopack.com roster lists Scott as **#1** and Vander Ploog as #4 TE — treat athletics roster numbers as canonical for full roster; DC depth order as official for roles.
2. **Ka'Tron Evans NCAA eligibility** beyond Week 1 described as uncertain (PackInsider).
3. **Ondre Evans** (Georgia transfer CB) started Day 1 of camp but lost job to Sensabaugh and was off the two-deep for Virginia.
4. **Elijah Satchell** not on official two-deep but played snaps vs Virginia (PackInsider snap-count follow-up).
5. **Recruiting stars** largely Not listed; Brody Smith and Elijah Satchell noted as ~3* (247/On3 ~86) only.
6. Ourlads LB/CB order differs from official Virginia chart in places (e.g., Da'Kaari Nelson vs AJ Richardson as WLB) — **official Pack chart used**.
7. Some walk-ons / injured (Ourlads INJ list: Crowell, Hoffmann, Ritchey, Vann, Copper, McKenzie, Ferguson) may have limited availability — not fully cross-checked against Week 1 snaps.

## Data gaps

- **Stars:** Almost all players listed as **Not listed**. Official athletics roster does not publish recruiting stars; no complete verified 247/On3/ESPN composite scraped for every rostered player (transfers + walk-ons + returning classes). Do not invent.
- **Official depth chart:** None. Belichick policy; all starter boards are media projections.
- **Projection conflicts:** RB1 (June vs Hall), TE1 (Warren vs Thurman), OL interior assignments, FS order, nickel vs corner for Ade Willie differ across SI / Heel Tough / Ourlads.
- **Ourlads INJ note:** Jaxxon Warren and Jordan Washington listed as reserves/INJ on Ourlads ~Aug 29; verify availability game-to-game.
- **Duplicate jersey numbers:** Common (e.g., #0 June/Johnson, #9 Edwards/Abou Jaoude, #92 Alo-Suliafu/Burrell) — offense/defense sharing.
- **Height/weight:** Available on athletics site but omitted from requested columns.

**Summary:** 111 players | Depth chart: PROJECTED | Primary sources: goheels.com + SI Inside Carolina + Heel Tough + Ourlads

## Data gaps

- **Stars:** All **Not listed** — athletics site does not publish recruiting stars; no verified complete 247/On3/ESPN set applied to full roster.
- **Class labels:** Roster uses Sr./So./Fr./5th/6th (not always R- designations); left as published.
- **Transfer previous schools:** Partially noted where confirmed in beat coverage; many not on athletics hometown line.
- **Depth chart vs full roster:** Several rostered players (e.g., Kanye Thompson) not listed on Week 1 two-deep.
- **Name spelling:** SI used "Holden Gerriner" / "Ryan Caretta" / "DeMarco" / "Jiavanni"; athletics roster uses Geriner / Carretta / DeMarco / Jiavani — roster spelling preferred.

**Summary:** 113 players | Depth chart: OFFICIAL (Week 1) | Primary sources: pittsburghpanthers.com + SI + Week 1 notes

## Data Gaps

1. **Stars:** Vast majority Not listed — only 2026 HS signees with published 247 ratings filled. Transfer/HS composite for veterans not bulk-pulled.
2. **Wikipedia vs athletics mismatches:** Some Wikipedia positions/numbers differ (e.g., Jacquon Gibson listed WR on athletics, some secondary sources say RB historically); athletics site preferred.
3. **Alex Reisig / Zion Wilson:** Appear on Wikipedia roster scrape; not clearly on live virginiasports card list at fetch time — treat as possible gap/omission.
4. **Post-Week 0 depth updates:** Official chart is Week 0; Kam Robinson availability and RB rotation after NC State win may shift for Norfolk State (not yet published as of research).
5. **X WR starter:** Flores Jr. OR Coleman remains OR on official sheet; game notes did not name which started at X.

## Data gaps

1. **No full OFFICIAL program depth chart** released as of Sept 4, 2026 (Franklin said chart may wait until gameday). Non-OL/QB spots are PROJECTED.
2. **Recruiting stars** unavailable for most upperclassmen/transfers without inventing — marked **Not listed**. Only 2026 HS signees from CFBTrack/247 have stars here.
3. **WR3 / DE order / LB alignment** still contested across SI projections and Ourlads (e.g., Hilson vs Williams at DE; Heath vs Jackson at WR3).

5. **Isaiah Pina** (3★ TE in CFBTrack class list) not found on current hokiesports roster table — may not have enrolled or not listed; excluded.
